That is the design working.\n\nThe dishonest path that remains: **`classify_refusal_text` (`:196-206`) checks AUTHORIZATION markers first, and `\"does not exist\"` is an AUTHORIZATION marker (`:168`), while the docstring (`:19-22`) lists \"sub-account existence\" as VALIDATION.** Measured, not argued:\n\n```\ncwd C:/tmp/OPUS_DD06_SCRATCH   (interpreter as above)\nclassify_refusal_text('Sub-account 0xdead does not exist')   -> 'AUTHORIZATION'\nclassify_refusal_text('Sub account does not exist')          -> 'AUTHORIZATION'\nclassify_refusal_text('Insufficient balance for agent')      -> 'AUTHORIZATION'\nclassify_refusal_text('Must deposit before performing actions. User: 0xabc') -> 'UNCLASSIFIED'\nclassify_refusal_text('Insufficient balance for withdrawal.')-> 'VALIDATION'\nclassify_refusal_text('Error withdrawing from bridge')        -> 'UNCLASSIFIED'\n```\n\nEnd-to-end (`C:/tmp/OPUS_DD06_SCRATCH/reviewer_checks.py`, output `REVIEWER_CHECKS_OUT.txt`), a run with `--sub-account` naming an address that does not exist:\n\n```\n=== CLAIM A: a non-existent sub-account yields AUTHORIZATION-class DD-06 evidence ===\nresult            : DD06_REFUSALS_OBSERVED\nfinding           : None\nsubAccountTransfer: REFUSED AUTHORIZATION\nvenue text        : {\"response\": \"Sub-account [redacted-address] does not exist\", \"status\": \"err\"}\nCLAIM A HOLDS     : True\n```\n\n`DD06_REFUSALS_OBSERVED` is the outcome that, per packet ┬ğ4, lets the owner move the DD-06 register row off BLOCK to \"primary testnet evidence\". A mistyped or stale `--sub-account` value therefore manufactures a share of that verdict out of an arm the venue refused only because the destination does not exist. ÔåÆ **R-3**.\n\n### Item 4 ÔÇö control arm\n\n`control_order_size` (`:228-236`) delegates to the Bridge's own `round_hl_price` (`bridge/broker/hyperliquid.py:72-89`), which rounds **down** to `max(10^-(6-szDecimals), 10^(adjusted-4) [┬À10 when ÔëÑ 10 000])` and returns integral prices unchanged. I swept it rather than reasoning about it (`C:/tmp/OPUS_DD06_SCRATCH/sweep.py`, `sweep2.py`):\n\n```\n1 140 006 mids: every 0.1 step in [10 000.0, 200 000.9], szDecimals = 5\nchecks: significant figures Ôëñ 5 (or integral) ┬À decimals Ôëñ 1 ┬À 0 < px Ôëñ 0.9┬Àmid ┬À px ÔëÑ 0.998┬À0.9┬Àmid ┬À size a clean 10^-5 multiple\nviolations of the wire-validity checks: 0\nr1 mid 76974.0 -> (69270.0, 0.00016)          [test asserts exactly 69270.0 ÔÇö tests/ÔÇĞ:159]\nr1's rejected price round(76974.0*0.9, 1) = 69276.6 -> 6 significant figures\nr3's recorded price 68520.0 reproduced from mid 76133.4\nnotional over the whole sweep: min 10.499999999999998, max 12.249719999999998\n```\n\nSo the r1 rejection (`Price must be divisible by tick size`, 69276.6) **cannot recur for any mid ÔëÑ 10 000**: at szDecimals 5 every produced price has Ôëñ 5 significant figures and Ôëñ 1 decimal, and stays at or below 90 % of mid so it cannot fill. Confirmed live twice at the venue ÔÇö r2 `68463.0` and r3 `68520.0` both rested and cancelled. The one imperfection is that the notional lands *exactly* on `CONTROL_MIN_NOTIONAL_USD` for some mids and float rounding puts it 1.8e-15 below (mid 10416.7 ÔåÆ 9375.0 ├ù 0.00112 = `10.499999999999998`), which breaks the module's own asserted invariant (`tests/ÔÇĞ:158`) for mids the test does not sample. (Also, `control_order_size` raises `ZeroDivisionError` for absurd mids below ~0.001; it is inside the S1 `try` and aborts safely.)\n\n### Item 5 ÔÇö fixture tests vs reality\n\nThe brief asks what venue-shaped facts the fixtures still do not carry and whether any could make the next run misreport. ÔåÆ **R-1**.\n\n### Item 2 ÔÇö funds cannot leave silently\n\n- `STOP_ON_NOT_REFUSED = True` (`:56`) is consumed at `:462-465`: the first `NOT_REFUSED` arm sets `finding`, sets `result = \"DD06_FINDING_NOT_REFUSED\"`, records every later arm as `SKIPPED_AFTER_FINDING` and returns. Pinned by `tests/ÔÇĞ:248-270`; **M5 kills**. Verified in reality: r3's `spotSend` NOT_REFUSED stopped the sequence and `approveAgent` was never sent (`kvm2_dd06_probe_run_r3_OUTPUT.redacted.txt:115-120`).\n- withdraw3 amount `6.0` USDC (`:53`) with destination `account_address` (`:390-394`). Both pinned: the fake asserts `amount == probe.WITHDRAW_AMOUNT_USDC >= 6.0` and `destination == ACCOUNT` (`tests/ÔÇĞ:95`); **M6 and M10 each kill 3 tests**. r3's record shows `{\"amount\": 6.0, \"destination\": \"[account]\"}` (`ÔÇĞr3_OUTPUTÔÇĞ:89`).\n- The packet says it plainly, twice, in the owner's own document: \"if NOT refused, 6 test-USDC leave the venue to an address you control\" (`DD06_TESTNET_PROBE_STEP_PACKET_20260915.md:22`) and \"A NOT-refused `withdraw3` means 6 test-USDC **leave the venue** to the owner's own address on the bridge chain ÔÇö stated plainly, not hidden behind 'own address'\" (`:26`). Gemini F-1 is satisfied against bytes.\n- Caveat on the wording: `FUND_MOVING_ARMS` (`:55`) is **defined and never referenced**; the stop rule actually fires on *any* `NOT_REFUSED` arm, `approveAgent` included. Broader than documented, i.e. safe-direction ÔÇö but a declared control that is not the one running. ÔåÆ **N-1**.\n- Funds cannot leave *silently*. They can still leave: withdraw3 is the **first** arm (`:388`), so the one irreversible action runs before the two in-venue self-transfers whose non-refusal would have tripped the stop rule first. Running `usdSend`/`spotSend` before `withdraw3` would carry identical evidence value in the all-refused case and strictly less exposure in the falsification case. I am **not** filing this as REQUIRED: ┬ğ3 of the packet is the owner-approved order, and a reviewer does not silently re-cut an approved procedure. ÔåÆ **N-9**, for the owner to decide.\n\n### Item 3 ÔÇö classification honesty\n\nThe honest paths are real and work:\n- `classify_response` (`:143-152`): `status == \"err\"` ÔåÆ REFUSED; `status == \"ok\"` with a `statuses[].error` ÔåÆ REFUSED (`per_status_errors`, `:130-140`); clean `ok` ÔåÆ NOT_REFUSED; anything else ÔåÆ INCONCLUSIVE. Pinned by `tests/ÔÇĞ:162-185`.\n- `classify_exception` (`:155-162`): `ClientError` ÔåÆ REFUSED, `ServerError`/other ÔåÆ ERROR. Pinned by `tests/ÔÇĞ:313-327`.\n- The verdict gate (`:466-470`) requires `refused == len(arms) **and** authorization_refusals == len(arms)`; otherwise `DD06_INCONCLUSIVE`. **M9 dies** on `test_validation_shaped_refusal_makes_the_run_inconclusive` (`tests/ÔÇĞ:292-310`). So a *plainly* validation-shaped refusal cannot be counted as DD-06 evidence ÔÇö and r2 is the proof: the venue's real text `Must deposit before performing actions. User: <address>` classified `UNCLASSIFIED` on all four arms and the run reported `DD06_INCONCLUSIVE` rather than claiming evidence (`LEAD_READING_r2_20260916.md:11-14`). That is the design working.\n\nThe dishonest path that remains: **`classify_refusal_text` (`:196-206`) checks AUTHORIZATION markers first, and `\"does not exist\"` is an AUTHORIZATION marker (`:168`), while the docstring (`:19-22`) lists \"sub-account existence\" as VALIDATION.** Measured, not argued:\n\n```\ncwd C:/tmp/OPUS_DD06_SCRATCH   (interpreter as above)\nclassify_refusal_text('Sub-account 0xdead does not exist')   -> 'AUTHORIZATION'\nclassify_refusal_text('Sub account does not exist')          -> 'AUTHORIZATION'\nclassify_refusal_text('Insufficient balance for agent')      -> 'AUTHORIZATION'\nclassify_refusal_text('Must deposit before performing actions. User: 0xabc') -> 'UNCLASSIFIED'\nclassify_refusal_text('Insufficient balance for withdrawal.')-> 'VALIDATION'\nclassify_refusal_text('Error withdrawing from bridge')        -> 'UNCLASSIFIED'\n```\n\nEnd-to-end (`C:/tmp/OPUS_DD06_SCRATCH/reviewer_checks.py`, output `REVIEWER_CHECKS_OUT.txt`), a run with `--sub-account` naming an address that does not exist:\n\n```\n=== CLAIM A: a non-existent sub-account yields AUTHORIZATION-class DD-06 evidence ===\nresult            : DD06_REFUSALS_OBSERVED\nfinding           : None\nsubAccountTransfer: REFUSED AUTHORIZATION\nvenue text        : {\"response\": \"Sub-account [redacted-address] does not exist\", \"status\": \"err\"}\nCLAIM A HOLDS     : True\n```\n\n`DD06_REFUSALS_OBSERVED` is the outcome that, per packet ┬ğ4, lets the owner move the DD-06 register row off BLOCK to \"primary testnet evidence\". A mistyped or stale `--sub-account` value therefore manufactures a share of that verdict out of an arm the venue refused only because the destination does not exist. ÔåÆ **R-3**.\n\n### Item 4 ÔÇö control arm\n\n`control_order_size` (`:228-236`) delegates to the Bridge's own `round_hl_price` (`bridge/broker/hyperliquid.py:72-89`), which rounds **down** to `max(10^-(6-szDecimals), 10^(adjusted-4) [┬À10 when ÔëÑ 10 000])` and returns integral prices unchanged. I swept it rather than reasoning about it (`C:/tmp/OPUS_DD06_SCRATCH/sweep.py`, `sweep2.py`):\n\n```\n1 140 006 mids: every 0.1 step in [10 000.0, 200 000.9], szDecimals = 5\nchecks: significant figures Ôëñ 5 (or integral) ┬À decimals Ôëñ 1 ┬À 0 < px Ôëñ 0.9┬Àmid ┬À px ÔëÑ 0.998┬À0.9┬Àmid ┬À size a clean 10^-5 multiple\nviolations of the wire-validity checks: 0\nr1 mid 76974.0 -> (69270.0, 0.00016)          [test asserts exactly 69270.0 ÔÇö tests/ÔÇĞ:159]\nr1's rejected price round(76974.0*0.9, 1) = 69276.6 -> 6 significant figures\nr3's recorded price 68520.0 reproduced from mid 76133.4\nnotional over the whole sweep: min 10.499999999999998, max 12.249719999999998\n```\n\nSo the r1 rejection (`Price must be divisible by tick size`, 69276.6) **cannot recur for any mid ÔëÑ 10 000**: at szDecimals 5 every produced price has Ôëñ 5 significant figures and Ôëñ 1 decimal, and stays at or below 90 % of mid so it cannot fill. Confirmed live twice at the venue ÔÇö r2 `68463.0` and r3 `68520.0` both rested and cancelled. The one imperfection is that the notional lands *exactly* on `CONTROL_MIN_NOTIONAL_USD` for some mids and float rounding puts it 1.8e-15 below (mid 10416.7 ÔåÆ 9375.0 ├ù 0.00112 = `10.499999999999998`), which breaks the module's own asserted invariant (`tests/ÔÇĞ:158`) for mids the test does not sample. No venue impact ÔÇö the real floor is $10 and the constant carries $0.50 of margin. ÔåÆ **N-5**. (Also, `control_order_size` raises `ZeroDivisionError` for absurd mids below ~0.001; it is inside the S1 `try` and aborts safely.)\n\n### Item 5 ÔÇö fixture tests vs reality\n\nThe brief asks what venue-shaped facts the fixtures still do not carry and whether any could make the next run misreport. One of them already did, on **r3, with this exact candidate**:\n\n- **Whose funds moved is not in the record.** S0 reads `extra_agents`, `user_state` and `spot_user_state` for `account_address` only (`:283-333`) ÔÇö never for `wallet.address`, which `main` has in hand (`:570`, `:580`). So when an arm succeeds, the record says `\"finding\": \"spotSend was NOT refused for an agent wallet\"` and `\"result\": \"DD06_FINDING_NOT_REFUSED\"` beside the **master's** balance and nothing else. On r3 the truth was benign: the agent moved its **own** 1 USDC (agent 14.0 ÔåÆ 13.0, master 983.987457 ÔåÆ 984.987457; the venue ledger names `user = 0xfa06ÔÇĞ8504`, the agent ÔÇö `LEAD_PUBLIC_READS_after_r3.txt:2-3,9`). The disambiguation came entirely from a pre-written human rule plus two out-of-band public reads, not from the artefact the probe produces. Reproduced offline:\n  ```\n  === CLAIM C: the record of a NOT_REFUSED spotSend carries no agent-wallet balance ===\n  result : DD06_FINDING_NOT_REFUSED\n  finding: spotSend was NOT refused for an agent wallet\n  steps  : [S0_extra_agents, S0_user_state, S0_spot_user_state, S1_control_order, S1_control_cancel,\n            subAccountTransfer, withdraw3, usdSend, spotSend, approveAgent]\n  any step naming the AGENT wallet's own balance: []\n  CLAIM C HOLDS: True\n  ```\n  The author names the same fix in his own r3 reading (\"a slice that reads both balances before/after and classifies OWN_FUNDS_MOVED vs MASTER_FUNDS_MOVED\", `LEAD_READING_r3_20260916.md:30`) and did not put it in the candidate. ÔåÆ **R-4**.\n- **Facts the fixtures now carry correctly** (checked against the r2/r3 bytes, so I am not guessing): the `ok` + `statuses[].error` order-rejection shape (`tests/ÔÇĞ:162-185`, `:330-367`); the `{\"status\":\"err\",\"response\":\"<text>\"}` transfer-refusal shape; `approve_agent` returning a 2-tuple ÔÇö the real SDK signature is `approve_agent(self, name=None) -> Tuple[Any, str]`, so the discard at `:256-258` is right; `statuses: [\"success\"]` for cancel; a unified/classic account holding USDC in **spot** with perp `accountValue 0.0` (S0 spot read, `:311-333`, added in slice 3 ÔÇö r2/r3 both show it). Every `ExchangeLike`/`InfoLike` member matches the installed SDK 1-for-1 (`withdraw_from_bridge`, `usd_transfer`, `spot_transfer(amount, destination, token)`, `sub_account_transfer(sub_account_user, is_deposit, usd)`, `order`, `cancel`, `extra_agents`, `spot_user_state`, `all_mids`, `meta`); `spotSend` with the bare token string `\"USDC\"` is accepted on testnet (r3 proved it ÔÇö do not \"fix\" that).\n- **Facts still not carried, and what each would do to the next run**: (a) the `statuses` payload of a *successful* withdraw3/usdSend/spotSend ÔÇö the fixtures only ever return `{\"type\":\"default\"}`, which is what r3 actually returned, so this is now low risk; (b) a **filled** control order ÔÇö see N-3, the code would call it \"rests on the book\"; (c) sub-account refusal texts ÔÇö see R-3, the untested branch is the one that corrupts the verdict; (d) rate-limit/`429` `ClientError`s, which become REFUSED + UNCLASSIFIED ÔåÆ INCONCLUSIVE (safe); (e) a withdraw3 attempted with a **funded perp** balance, which no run has yet exercised (r3's withdraw3 died on the agent's 0 perp balance with the opaque `Error withdrawing from bridge`) ÔÇö so \"the agent cannot withdraw with funds present\" is still not tested; (f) mainnet parity, which the packet correctly refuses to claim.\n\n### Item 6 ÔÇö scope and suite\n\nExactly two files, both new, 1016 insertions, zero deletions (┬ğ1). Focused suite re-run by me: **16 passed** (┬ğ2). Full Bridge suite: I did **not** re-run it (see NOT VERIFIED) and rely on `LEAD_PYTEST_full_bridge_slice3.txt`, whose bytes read `1614 passed, 1 skipped, 1 warning in 166.08s (0:02:46)` / `full-suite-exit=0` ÔÇö note the brief's citation omits the `1 skipped`. `LEAD_RUFF_slice3.txt` = `All checks passed!`; `LEAD_GUARD_slice3.txt` = `RESULT: PASS` (the two \"dirty\" entries in that guard log are the candidate's own files pre-commit).\n\n---\n\n## 4. Findings\n\n### REQUIRED\n\n**R-1 ÔÇö the mainnet boundary, the master-key call and the record's last-resort hex guard have zero test coverage; four mutants survive a green suite.**\n`TESTNET_URL` (`tools/dd06_agent_withdraw_probe.py:47`), the `refuse_master_key(...)` call in `main` (`:572`), `del api_key` (`:571`) and the two `write_record` surviving-hex refusals (`:509-514`) are each deletable/alterable with `16 passed` still printed (M1-M4, ┬ğ3 item 1). The brief pre-wrote the disposition for this case; it is also this repo's own precedent (P0-31 OD-7: a sole guard with no test). Cheapest fixes that would kill each mutant: assert `plan_text()` contains the literal testnet host (kills M2 at zero cost); one test that monkeypatches `bridge.settings.resolve_hyperliquid_credentials` and `eth_account.Account.from_key` and asserts `main` exits 3 when the derived address equals the account address (kills M3); one test feeding `write_record` a `ProbeRecord` whose step data carries a raw 64-hex string and asserting `ProbeRefused` (kills M4). `del api_key` is the weakest of the four in substance ÔÇö `Account.from_key` keeps the key material inside `wallet` for the whole run, so the line is a gesture, not a control; either pin it or drop the claim.\n\n**R-2 ÔÇö the test suite calls the real `main()`, and only one refusal keeps it offline; remove that refusal and `pytest` becomes a live venue run with the operator's real credential.**\n`tests/test_dd06_agent_withdraw_probe.py:407-412` invokes `probe.main([\"--run-id\",\"x\",\"--out\",\"unused\",\"--network\",\"testnet\"])` with nothing stubbed ÔÇö no patch of `resolve_hyperliquid_credentials`, `Info`, or `Exchange`. Demonstrated, not theorised: ┬ğ0. On this host, `HKEY_CURRENT_USER\\Environment` holds both `HL_ACCOUNT_ADDRESS` (42 chars) and `HL_API_WALLET_KEY` (66 chars), so resolution succeeds anywhere, not only on KVM2. Two independent fixes, both cheap: (1) in that test, monkeypatch the credential resolver and the two SDK constructors so the real branch can never dial; (2) in `main`, require a second, explicit execution token (e.g. `DD06_PROBE_RUN_TOKEN=<run-id>` in the environment, or an `--i-have-the-owners-word` flag) *before* constructing `Info`/`Exchange`, so no single-line change can arm the tool. (2) also closes N-6.\n\n**R-3 ÔÇö a refusal that only says the destination does not exist is counted as AUTHORIZATION-class DD-06 evidence, contradicting the module's own specification.**\n`classify_refusal_text` (`:196-206`) tests AUTHORIZATION markers first and `\"does not exist\"` is one (`:168`), while the docstring (`:19-22`) and the packet (`:26`) both put \"sub-account existence\" in VALIDATION. With `--sub-account` naming a non-existent address the run returns `DD06_REFUSALS_OBSERVED` ÔÇö the verdict that moves the register row ÔÇö with that arm's `refusal_class: AUTHORIZATION` (Claim A, ┬ğ3 item 3). Fix: check VALIDATION markers first for existence/precondition wording, or narrow the AUTHORIZATION marker to the venue's actual authorization sentence (`\"user or api wallet\"` + `\"does not exist\"`), and add the two failing inputs to `test_refusal_text_classes` (`tests/ÔÇĞ:273-289`).\n\n**R-4 ÔÇö the record cannot support the verdict it prints: on a `NOT_REFUSED` fund arm it says \"was NOT refused for an agent wallet\" without ever reading the agent wallet's own balance.**\n`:283-333` reads state only for `account_address`; `:460` writes the finding text. This exact code printed `DD06_FINDING_NOT_REFUSED` / `\"spotSend was NOT refused for an agent wallet\"` on r3 while the truth was that the agent had moved its **own** 1 USDC (┬ğ3 item 5, Claim C). A reader of `DD06_PROBE_RECORD.json` alone would conclude the restriction was falsified. Fix, using data `main` already holds: add `S0_agent_user_state` / `S0_agent_spot_user_state` for `wallet.address`, re-read both wallets after any `NOT_REFUSED` fund arm, and split the result label into `DD06_FINDING_OWN_FUNDS_MOVED` vs `DD06_FINDING_MASTER_FUNDS_MOVED` (the author's own prescription, `LEAD_READING_r3_20260916.md:30`). Until then, no record this probe writes can close DD-06 on its own; the current \"VERIFIED on testnet\" reading rests on `LEAD_PUBLIC_READS_after_r3.txt`, which I did verify against bytes (`:2-3`, `:9`) and which does support the Lead's conclusion.\n\n### NIT\n\n- **N-1** `FUND_MOVING_ARMS` (`:55`) is defined and never used; the stop rule at `:459-465` fires on *any* `NOT_REFUSED` arm (safe-direction), so the docstring's \"if a fund-moving arm is NOT refused\" (`:19-20`) describes a control that does not exist as written. Delete the constant or use it.\n- **N-2** `_attempt` catches `BaseException` (`:245`), so a `KeyboardInterrupt` during an arm is recorded as `ERROR` and the loop proceeds to the **next** fund-moving arm ÔÇö the operator's abort gesture cannot stop the sequence. Re-raise `KeyboardInterrupt`/`SystemExit`.\n- **N-3** `_resting_oid` (`:479-485`) regex-matches any `\"oid\": N` in the redacted response, including a `filled` status. A filled control order (a ~10 % flash move while the bid rests) therefore yields `ABORTED_CONTROL_CANCEL_FAILED` with `\"control order rests on the book ÔÇĞ cancel it by hand\"` ÔÇö the wrong remedy for an open position. Verified offline (Claim B, `REVIEWER_CHECKS_OUT.txt`). The packet's abort conditions (`:25`) do tell the owner to flatten, so this is a wording/parse nit, not an exposure. Fix: require the `resting` key in `statuses[0]`.\n- **N-4** `_HEX40` (`:59`) requires the `0x` prefix, so a bare 40-hex address passes both `redact` and the `write_record` guard. Addresses are public; hygiene only.\n- **N-5** min-notional lands exactly on the constant for some mids and float rounding puts it 1.8e-15 below (┬ğ3 item 4), breaking the suite's own `>=` assertion for unsampled mids. Use a small margin (`* 1.01`) or compare with a tolerance.\n- **N-6** nothing in the tool binds execution to KVM2-P4-03 although packet ┬ğ3 does; `--network testnet` is the *default*, so the tool is one command away from running wherever `HL_*` exists (proven by ┬ğ0, from the Windows workstation). Covered by fix (2) of R-2.\n- **N-7** `--include-usd-class-transfer` (`:434-441`, `:550`) has no test and `usd_class_transfer` is absent from the `ExchangeLike` protocol (`:89-106`) ÔÇö the real SDK does have `usd_class_transfer(amount, to_perp)`, so only the flag's plumbing is unverified.\n- **N-8** brief/record drift: the brief's subject line says the probe \"ran once on testnet\"; three runs exist (┬ğ1). No code impact; flagged so the Friday Sol lane is not briefed on a stale premise.\n- **N-9** arm order: `withdraw3`, the only arm that can move funds **off** the venue, runs first (`:388`), ahead of the two in-venue self-transfers whose non-refusal would trip the stop rule and make the withdrawal unnecessary. Reordering is strictly safer with identical evidence value in the all-refused case ÔÇö but ┬ğ3 of the packet is the owner-approved order, so this is the owner's call, not a reviewer's rewrite.\n\n### What is sound (so the repair does not erase it)\n\nRedaction and the write-once record are real and pinned (M7 dies; the on-host r2/r3 records are address-free). The stop rule is real and has fired at the venue. The refusal-class gate genuinely refuses to convert a non-authorization refusal into DD-06 evidence ÔÇö r2's real venue text landed in `UNCLASSIFIED` and the run reported `DD06_INCONCLUSIVE`. The control arm's price arithmetic is now correct across 1.14 M mids and has been accepted twice by the live venue.
